## Your Tasks

### Task 1 — Relationship Analysis

Identify ALL relationships between tables:
- Explicit FK constraints (listed above)
- Inferred from column naming: `<table>_id` → `<table>.id`, `<table>_code` → `<table>.code`, etc.
- Junction/pivot tables (tables with exactly 2 FK-like columns suggesting many-to-many)
- Self-referential relationships (e.g. `parent_id` in the same table)

### Task 2 — Smart Queries JSON

Output a valid JSON file with **exactly** this structure. This file will be loaded directly into the database admin tool:

```json
{
  "version": "1.0",
  "generatedAt": "2026-06-08T16:32:29.425Z",
  "connection": {
    "type": "postgresql",
    "host": "172.22.150.176",
    "port": 5432,
    "database": "Contelo"
  },
```

**Instructions for filling the JSON:**
- `entities[table].displayField`: the column most useful for display (name, title, email, code, description)
- `entities[table].description`: 1-sentence description of what this table represents in the business domain
- `entities[table].columns[col].label`: human-readable label for the column
- `entities[table].columns[col].searchable`: true if this column is useful for text search
- `entities[table].relations`: all relationships this entity has (hasMany, belongsTo, hasOne, manyToMany)
- `smartTemplates`: 3-5 useful queries per entity showing joins, aggregations, and lookups
- `relationships[].confidence`: "explicit" if FK-declared, "inferred" if guessed from naming
- `diagram.mermaid`: a valid Mermaid ER diagram string

### Task 3 — Schema Report

Write a concise (< 400 words) natural-language description of the data model:
- What domain/application this database appears to serve
- Key entities and their roles
- Main data flows (e.g. "a User places Orders, each Order has OrderItems linked to Products")
- Any notable patterns (audit tables, lookup/reference tables, soft deletes, multi-tenancy, etc.)

### Task 4 — Mermaid ER Diagram

Generate a `erDiagram` block showing all entities and their relationships.
Use standard Mermaid ER notation: `||--o{`, `}o--||`, `||--||`, etc.
